| Mizuki | Hey there, everyone! Morning! | talk |

|  | bgm00000 | Sound |
| Mizuki | That day, a female student was passing through the courtyard in order to run an errand for a teacher. | talk |
|  | bgm00027 | Sound |
| Mizuki | The courtyard was empty because it was evening...<br>Yet, the student could hear a woman wailing. | talk |
| An | She was probably just imagining things...<br>Maybe it was actually the voice of someone doing club activities? | talk |
| Mizuki | If only...<br>Did you know that the courtyard of this school used to be a cemetery? | talk |
| An | No way?! | talk |
| Mizuki | The wailing sounded soft at first, but then it got louder and louder, until it felt like there was someone right next to her... | talk |
| An | Gulp... | talk |
| Mizuki | The student steeled herself and turned around.<br>But there was no one there. | talk |
| Mizuki | Then, as she was breathing a sigh of relief, she saw it. | talk |
| An | S-Saw what? | talk |
| Mizuki | A pale, white hand protruding out from the ground, wrapped around her ankle! | talk |
|  | bgm00000 | Sound |
| Mizuki | And just like that! | talk |
| An | Ah?! | talk |
| Mizuki | The girl fainted there and then. And when she woke up, she was lying on a bed in the nurse's office. The end♪ | talk |
| An | Th-That means, the student next door really fainted in the courtyard because of that ghost? | talk |
| Mizuki | Wait a minute? Why's your face so pale?<br>Are you maybe actually afraid of ghosts? | talk |
|  | bgm00018 | Sound |

| Mizuki | Thank you!<br>See you after school, then! | talk |

| Toya | Does that have something to do with this idea of yours...? | talk |
| An | Yup, you got it! | talk |
| An | We were originally two separate pairs, right? We really only became a foursome just recently if you think about it. | talk |
| An | That's why I want us to do as many events as possible as a complete team. | talk |
| Toya | I see.<br>But why just battles? | talk |
| An | To keep honing our skills! | talk |
| An | Battle events have winners and losers, right? | talk |
| An | And no one participates in these unless they feel like they can win! | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, everyone's at each other's throats thinking that they'll be the ones that get the crowd going the most. | talk |

| Kohane | A singing style? | talk |

| An | I know a lot about solo artists and performers, but not much about teams... | talk |

| Akito | And that's what you meant by us finding a singing style, huh...? | talk |

| An | That was so much fun! It's so great just singing your heart out on stage! | talk |
| An | Thanks again, everyone. Getting my wish granted made this the best birthday ever! | talk |
| Rin | I'm so glad to hear it! Happy birthday, An! | talk |
| An | But you know, I don't think I'm done singing just yet. | talk |
| Akito | Huh? | talk |
| An | I mean, just look at this amazing stage! Isn't it kind of a waste to be done already? | talk |
| Toya | True, you make a good point. | talk |
| An | So, welcome to free singing time, everyone! | talk |
| Len | Ooh, nice! | talk |
| Rin | Okay, I'll sing some of An's favorite songs, then! | talk |
| An | Hey, whoa! How about letting the birthday girl go first?! | talk |
| Kohane | Oh, then... Can I sing with you? | talk |
| An | Huh? | talk |
| Kohane | We faced off earlier, so now I'd like to sing by your side... Would that be okay? | talk |
| An | Of course! Let's have everyone listen to our song! | talk |

| Haruka | Happy birthday, An. I was so surprised when you replied to my birthday message with like three times as many messages. | talk |

| Minori | Hehehe, we sent so many messages back and forth! | talk |
| Minori | But just texting doesn't feel like enough of a celebration... Let's do something special when you get back home to Japan! | talk |
